./docs/audit/crash-test-audit.md:5:- **Analysis:** The previous crash harness implementation in `scripts/crash-test` (commit `e13a14e`) utilized an in-process multi-threaded simulation harness where "crash" was signaled via `crashed.set()` on a `threading.Event` and "restart" was simulated with `time.sleep()`. Consequently, the reported 85.63 ms recovery time represented an in-memory thread synchronization delay rather than the real-world OS process restart, JVM class loading, Spring Boot context initialization, and Flyway migration time of a real separate process.
